Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the astrophysics framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 132. The resulting operator achieves bound O(593/n^3).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 132 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the astrophysics structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 593. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let the astrophysics structure S satisfy the conditions of structural decomposition framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 162. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 815 and converges at rate O(1/n^3).
